<p id="mwCQ"><b id="mwCg"><span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt25" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwCw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt25" id="mwDA"/></b> ("Supplicating Trust")<sup about="#mwt27" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Wooden_1-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Wooden"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Wooden-1" id="mwDQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwDg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwDw">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwEA">]</span></span></a></sup> is a 2023 declaration on <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholic_theology" title="Catholic theology" id="mwEQ">Catholic doctrine</a> that allows <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_Church" title="Latin Church" id="mwEg">Latin</a><sup about="#mwt30" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-2"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2" id="mwEw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwFA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFQ">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFg">]</span></span></a></sup> Catholic <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Priesthood_in_the_Catholic_Church" title="Priesthood in the Catholic Church" id="mwFw">priests</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deacon#Catholicism" title="Deacon" id="mwGA">deacons</a><sup about="#mwt33" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-3"}}'><a href="#cite_note-3" id="mwGQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwGg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwGw">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwHA">]</span></span></a></sup> to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blessing_in_the_Catholic_Church" title="Blessing in the Catholic Church" id="mwHQ">bless</a> couples who are not <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marriage_in_the_Catholic_Church" title="Marriage in the Catholic Church" id="mwHg">married according to church teaching</a>, including individuals in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Same-sex_relationship" title="Same-sex relationship" id="mwHw">same-sex relationships</a>.<sup about="#mwt34" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:5_4-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":5"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:5-4" id="mwIA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwIQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwIg">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwIw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt37" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-5"}}'><a href="#cite_note-5" id="mwJA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwJQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJg">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt40" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:0_6-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":0"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:0-6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:0-6" id="mwKA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwKQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwKg">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwKw">]</span></span></a></sup> Subtitled "On the Pastoral Meaning of Blessings", the document is dated 18 December 2023 and was released on the same day. <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt26" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwLA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt26" id="mwLQ"/> was issued by the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Holy_See" title="Holy See" id="mwLg">Holy See</a>'s <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dicastery_for_the_Doctrine_of_the_Faith" title="Dicastery for the Doctrine of the Faith" id="mwLw">Dicastery for the Doctrine of the Faith</a> (DDF) and approved with a signature by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_Francis" title="Pope Francis" id="mwMA">Pope Francis</a>.<sup about="#mwt43" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-7" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-7" id="mwMQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwMg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwMw">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt46" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:9_8-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":9"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:9-8"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:9-8" id="mwNQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwNg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNw">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwOA">]</span></span></a></sup></p> 

<p id="mwOQ">It was the first declaration issued by the DDF since <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt47" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwOg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"[[Dominus Iesus]]"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dominus_Iesus" title="Dominus Iesus">Dominus Iesus</a></i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt47" id="mwOw"/> in 2000.<sup about="#mwt50" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Coppen1_9-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Coppen1"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Coppen1-9"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Coppen1-9" id="mwPA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwPQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwPg">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwPw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwQA"><span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt51" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwQQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt51" id="mwQg"/> has been widely interpreted.<sup about="#mwt58" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:8_10-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":8"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:8-10"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:8-10" id="mwQw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwRA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRQ">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRg">]</span></span></a></sup> Francis advised that the Holy See's bureaucrats should avoid "rigid ideological positions" three days after the document was issued.<sup about="#mwt61" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:6_11-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":6"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:6-11"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:6-11" id="mwRw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwSA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSQ">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSg">]</span></span></a></sup> Prefect of the DDF <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Víctor_Manuel_Fernández" title="Víctor Manuel Fernández" id="mwSw">Víctor Manuel Fernández</a> later said in an interview that the declaration did not permit blessing the unions, with a DDF press release in January 2024 repeating this. While most coverage reported that <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt52" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwTA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt52" id="mwTQ"/> reversed a 2021 <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt53" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwTg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"[[Responsa|responsum ad dubium]]"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Responsa" title="Responsa">responsum ad dubium</a></i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt53" id="mwTw"/> from the DDF's predecessor,<sup about="#mwt62" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:6_11-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:6-11" id="mwUA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwUQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUg">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt63" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:10_12-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":10"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:10-12" id="mwVA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwVQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVg">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt64" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:3_13-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":3"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:3-13" id="mwWA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwWQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWg">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt65" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:14_14-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":14"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:14-14" id="mwXA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwXQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwXg">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwXw">]</span></span></a></sup> which ruled that the Church does not have the "power to give the blessing to unions of persons of the same sex",<sup about="#mwt68" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:15_15-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":15"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:15-15"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:15-15" id="mwYA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwYQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYg">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYw">]</span></span></a></sup> other commentators said that the 2021 ruling was still effective.<sup about="#mwt69" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:16_16-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":16"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:16-16" id="mwZA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwZQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwZg">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwZw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt70" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:17_17-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":17"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:17-17" id="mwaA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwaQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwag">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwaw">]</span></span></a></sup> In its introduction, <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt54" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwbA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt54" id="mwbQ"/> describes itself as "offering new clarifications" on the 2021 <i id="mwbg"><span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt55" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwbw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"responsum"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">responsum</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt55" id="mwcA"/></i>.<sup about="#mwt71" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:5_4-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":5"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:5-4" id="mwcQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwcg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwcw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwdA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" id="mwdQ" data-mw='{"autoGenerated":true}'/></section><section data-mw-section-id="1" id="mwdg" aria-labelledby="Background"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Background">Background</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Fiducia_supplicans&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Background"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt72" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwdw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Further","href":"./Template:Further"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Pope Francis and LGBT topics"},"2":{"wt":"Blessing of same-sex unions in Christian churches#Catholic Church"}},"i":0}}]}'><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1368532237" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt73" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Hatnote/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'>.mw-parser-output .hatnote{font-style:italic}.mw-parser-output div.hatnote{padding-left:1.6em;margin-bottom:0.5em}.mw-parser-output .hatnote i{font-style:normal}.mw-parser-output .hatnote+.mw-empty-elt+.hatnote{margin-top:-0.5em}@media print{body.ns-0 .mw-parser-output .hatnote{display:none!important}}</style></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt72" id="mweA">Further information: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_Francis_and_LGBT_topics" title="Pope Francis and LGBT topics" class="mw-redirect">Pope Francis and LGBT topics</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blessing_of_same-sex_unions_in_Christian_churches#Catholic_Church" title="Blessing of same-sex unions in Christian churches">Blessing of same-sex unions in Christian churches §<span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>Catholic Church</a></div>

<p id="mweQ">Before 2021, several dioceses had permitted the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blessing_in_the_Catholic_Church" title="Blessing in the Catholic Church" id="mweg">blessing</a> of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Same-sex_relationship" title="Same-sex relationship" id="mwew">same-sex couples</a>, including <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diocese_of_Linz" title="Diocese of Linz" id="mwfA">Linz</a> in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Austria" title="Austria" id="mwfQ">Austria</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diocese_of_Basel" title="Diocese of Basel" id="mwfg">Basel</a> in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Switzerland" title="Switzerland" id="mwfw">Switzerland</a>.<sup about="#mwt80" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-18" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-18"}}'><a href="#cite_note-18" id="mwgA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwgQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwgg">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwgw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt83" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-19" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-19"}}'><a href="#cite_note-19" id="mwhA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwhQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwhg">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwhw">]</span></span></a></sup> On 15 March 2021, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Congregation_for_the_Doctrine_of_the_Faith" title="Congregation for the Doctrine of the Faith" class="mw-redirect" id="mwiA">Congregation for the Doctrine of the Faith</a> (renamed the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dicastery_for_the_Doctrine_of_the_Faith" title="Dicastery for the Doctrine of the Faith" id="mwiQ">Dicastery for the Doctrine of the Faith</a> in 2022) issued a <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt74" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwig" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"[[Responsa|responsum ad dubium]]"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Responsa" title="Responsa">responsum ad dubium</a></i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt74" id="mwiw"/> ("response to doubt") that responded in the negative to the question of whether the Church has "the power to give the blessing to unions of persons of the same sex".<sup about="#mwt84" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:15_15-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":15"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:15-15" id="mwjA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwjQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwjg">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwjw">]</span></span></a></sup> While most coverage reported that <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt75" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwkA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt75" id="mwkQ"/> reversed this 2021 decision,<sup about="#mwt85" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:6_11-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:6-11" id="mwkg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwkw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwlA">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwlQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt86" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:10_12-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":10"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:10-12" id="mwlg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwlw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwmA">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwmQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt89" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:3_13-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":3"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:3-13"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:3-13" id="mwmg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwmw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwnA">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwnQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt92" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:14_14-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":14"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:14-14"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:14-14" id="mwng"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwnw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwoA">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwoQ">]</span></span></a></sup> other commentators said that the 2021 ruling was still effective.<sup about="#mwt95" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:16_16-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":16"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:16-16"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:16-16" id="mwog"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwow"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwpA">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwpQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt98" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:17_17-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":17"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:17-17"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:17-17" id="mwpg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwpw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwqA">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwqQ">]</span></span></a></sup> In its introduction, <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt76" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwqg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt76" id="mwqw"/> describes itself as "offering new clarifications" on the 2021 <i id="mwrA"><span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt77" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwrQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"responsum"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">responsum</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt77" id="mwrg"/></i>.<sup about="#mwt99" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:5_4-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":5"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:5-4" id="mwrw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwsA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwsQ">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwsg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwsw">Notwithstanding the 2021 <i id="mwtA"><span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt100" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwtQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"responsum"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">responsum</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt100" id="mwtg"/></i>, several <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Episcopal_conference" title="Episcopal conference" id="mwtw">bishops' conferences</a> had moved towards blessings for same-sex unions. In September 2022, over 80% of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/German_Bishops'_Conference" title="German Bishops' Conference" id="mwuA">German bishops</a> at the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Synodal_Way" title="Synodal Way" id="mwuQ">Synodal Way</a> supported a document calling for a "re-evaluation of homosexuality" and modifying the <i id="mwug"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catechism_of_the_Catholic_Church" title="Catechism of the Catholic Church" id="mwuw">Catechism of the Catholic Church</a></i>,<sup about="#mwt103" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-synodalerweg_20-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"synodalerweg"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-synodalerweg-20"}}'><a href="#cite_note-synodalerweg-20" id="mwvA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwvQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwvg">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwvw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt106" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-21" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-21"}}'><a href="#cite_note-21" id="mwwA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwwQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwwg">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwww">]</span></span></a></sup> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flemish_people" title="Flemish people" id="mwxA">Flemish</a> bishops of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Episcopal_Conference_of_Belgium" title="Episcopal Conference of Belgium" id="mwxQ">Episcopal Conference of Belgium</a> published a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholic_liturgy" title="Catholic liturgy" id="mwxg">liturgical</a> document for the blessing of same-sex unions.<sup about="#mwt109" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-22" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-22"}}'><a href="#cite_note-22" id="mwxw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwyA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwyQ">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwyg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt112" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-23" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-23"}}'><a href="#cite_note-23" id="mwyw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwzA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwzQ">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwzg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt115" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-24" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-24"}}'><a href="#cite_note-24" id="mwzw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw0A"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw0Q">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw0g">]</span></span></a></sup> After the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Synodal_Path" title="Synodal Path" class="mw-redirect" id="mw0w">Synodal Path</a>, the first German dioceses started blessing ceremonies for same-sex couples in March 2023, including the dioceses of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diocese_of_Osnabrück" title="Diocese of Osnabrück" id="mw1A">Osnabrück</a>,<sup about="#mwt118" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-25" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-25"}}'><a href="#cite_note-25" id="mw1Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw1g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw1w">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw2A">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt120" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-26" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-26"}}'><a href="#cite_note-26" id="mw2Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw2g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw2w">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw3A">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diocese_of_Essen" title="Diocese of Essen" id="mw3Q">Essen</a>,<sup about="#mwt124" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-neuesruhrwort.de_27-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"neuesruhrwort.de"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-neuesruhrwort.de-27"}}'><a href="#cite_note-neuesruhrwort.de-27" id="mw3g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw3w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw4A">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw4Q">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diocese_of_Speyer" title="Diocese of Speyer" id="mw4g">Speyer</a>,<sup about="#mwt127" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-28" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-28"}}'><a href="#cite_note-28" id="mw4w"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw5A"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw5Q">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw5g">]</span></span></a></sup> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Archdiocese_of_Berlin" title="Archdiocese of Berlin" id="mw5w">Berlin</a>.<sup about="#mwt130" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-29" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-29"}}'><a href="#cite_note-29" id="mw6A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw6Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw6g">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw6w">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mw7A">On 25 September 2023, in another <i id="mw7Q">responsum</i> to conservative <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cardinal_(Catholic_Church)" title="Cardinal (Catholic Church)" id="mw7g">cardinals</a> before the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sixteenth_Ordinary_General_Assembly_of_the_Synod_of_Bishops" title="Sixteenth Ordinary General Assembly of the Synod of Bishops" class="mw-redirect" id="mw7w">16th World Synod of Bishops</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_Francis" title="Pope Francis" id="mw8A">Pope Francis</a> signalled the Church's openness to blessings for gay couples as long as they did not misrepresent the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marriage_in_the_Catholic_Church" title="Marriage in the Catholic Church" id="mw8Q">Catholic view of marriage</a> as between one man and one woman.<sup about="#mwt133" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-McElwee-2023_30-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"McElwee-2023"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-McElwee-2023-30"}}'><a href="#cite_note-McElwee-2023-30" id="mw8g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw8w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw9A">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw9Q">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt136" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Allen-20232_31-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Allen-20232"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Allen-20232-31"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Allen-20232-31" id="mw9g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw9w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw-A">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw-Q">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="2" id="mw-g" aria-labelledby="Contents"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Contents">Contents</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Fiducia_supplicans&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Contents"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mw-w"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Pope_Francis_Korea_Haemi_Castle_19_(4x5_cropped).jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mw_A"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Pope_Francis_Korea_Haemi_Castle_19_(4x5_cropped).jpg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8b/Pope_Francis_Korea_Haemi_Castle_19_%284x5_cropped%29.jpg/250px-Pope_Francis_Korea_Haemi_Castle_19_%284x5_cropped%29.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8b/Pope_Francis_Korea_Haemi_Castle_19_%284x5_cropped%29.jpg/500px-Pope_Francis_Korea_Haemi_Castle_19_%284x5_cropped%29.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" alt="Photograph of Pope Francis" data-file-width="1734" data-file-height="2174" data-file-type="bitmap" height="238" width="190" class="mw-file-element mw-file-upright" style="--mw-file-upright: 0.75" id="mw_Q"/></a><figcaption id="mw_g"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_Francis" title="Pope Francis" id="mw_w">Pope Francis</a> (pictured in 2014) signed the declaration.<sup about="#mwt137" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:9_8-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":9"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:9-8" id="mwAQA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAQE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQI">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQM">]</span></span></a></sup></figcaption></figure>

<p id="mwAQQ"><span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt138" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAQU" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt138" id="mwAQY"/> provides clarification and reforms on the Catholic Church's treatment of "irregular relationships", that is, those who establish a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Monogamy" title="Monogamy" id="mwAQc">monogamous</a> and emotional bond that lasts over time and have not contracted a Catholic marriage. Notably, it allows <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Priesthood_in_the_Catholic_Church" title="Priesthood in the Catholic Church" id="mwAQg">Catholic priests</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deacon" title="Deacon" id="mwAQk">deacons</a> to perform "spontaneous blessings" of same-sex couples, as well as opposite-sex couples who are not married, and civilly married couples at least one party of which was previously divorced but has not received an <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Annulment_(Catholic_Church)" title="Annulment (Catholic Church)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAQo">annulment</a>.<sup about="#mwt141" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:5_4-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":5"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:5-4"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:5-4" id="mwAQs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAQw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQ0">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQ4">]</span></span></a></sup></p> 

<p id="mwAQ8">The document details that this type of informal and spontaneous blessing is neither a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sacraments_of_the_Catholic_Church" title="Sacraments of the Catholic Church" id="mwARA">sacrament</a> nor a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rite_(Christianity)" title="Rite (Christianity)" id="mwARE">rite</a> of the Catholic Church, so no special <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ceremony" title="Ceremony" id="mwARI">ceremony</a> is performed for it.<sup about="#mwt144" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Wooden_1-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Wooden"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Wooden-1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Wooden-1" id="mwARM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwARQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARU">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARY">]</span></span></a></sup> The document maintains that sexual relations are <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Validity_and_liceity_(Catholic_Church)" title="Validity and liceity (Catholic Church)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwARc">licit</a> only within marriage, so heterosexual couples are urged to marry and not consider this blessing as an alternative to marriage.<sup about="#mwt147" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-32" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-32"}}'><a href="#cite_note-32" id="mwARg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwARk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARo">[</span>32<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARs">]</span></span></a></sup> All <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Extramarital_sex" title="Extramarital sex" id="mwARw">extramarital sexual relations</a> are considered to be <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christian_views_on_sin" title="Christian views on sin" id="mwAR0">sinful</a> by the Church and continue to be so, leading to the implication that the nature of the ideal <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Affect_(psychology)" title="Affect (psychology)" id="mwAR4">affect</a> existing between two persons in a same-sex relationship is chaste-affection.<sup about="#mwt150" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-33" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-33"}}'><a href="#cite_note-33" id="mwAR8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwASA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASE">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASI">]</span></span></a></sup> While the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_attraction" title="Sexual attraction" id="mwASM">sexual attraction</a> between two people of the same sex cannot be legitimized, it is not a sin according to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholic_theology_of_sexuality" title="Catholic theology of sexuality" id="mwASQ">sexual morality</a> of the Catholic Church, but <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Homosexual_sexual_practices" title="Homosexual sexual practices" class="mw-redirect" id="mwASU">homosexual acts</a> are sinful.<sup about="#mwt153" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:10_12-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":10"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:10-12"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:10-12" id="mwASY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwASc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASg">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASk">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt156" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-34" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-34"}}'><a href="#cite_note-34" id="mwASo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwASs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASw">[</span>34<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAS0">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAS4"><span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt157" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAS8" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt157" id="mwATA"/> does not provide for changes with respect to the institution of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marriage_in_the_Catholic_Church" title="Marriage in the Catholic Church" id="mwATE">marriage in the Catholic Church</a>.<sup about="#mwt160" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-35" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-35"}}'><a href="#cite_note-35" id="mwATI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwATM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATQ">[</span>35<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATU">]</span></span></a></sup> Marriage is still understood only as the union between a man and a woman, to the exclusion of all kinds of marriages that are not <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heterosexuality" title="Heterosexuality" id="mwATY">heterosexual</a> and monogamous, such as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Same-sex_marriage" title="Same-sex marriage" id="mwATc">same-sex marriage</a>, as well as any kind of heterosexual or bisexual <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bigamy" title="Bigamy" id="mwATg">bigamy</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Polygamy" title="Polygamy" id="mwATk">polygamy</a>.<sup about="#mwt163" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-36" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-36"}}'><a href="#cite_note-36" id="mwATo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwATs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATw">[</span>36<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAT0">]</span></span></a></sup> In a December 2023 interview, Cardinal <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Víctor_Manuel_Fernández" title="Víctor Manuel Fernández" id="mwAT4">Víctor Manuel Fernández</a>, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prefect" title="Prefect" id="mwAT8">prefect</a> of the DDF, explained that such blessings applied to couples and that "the union is not blessed, for the reasons that the declaration repeatedly explains about the true meaning of Christian marriage and sexual relations".<sup about="#mwt166" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Interview_37-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Interview"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Interview-37"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Interview-37" id="mwAUA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAUE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUI">[</span>37<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="3" id="mwAUQ" aria-labelledby="Press_release"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Press_release">Press release</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Fiducia_supplicans&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Press release"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAUU">On 4 January 2024, the DDF published a press release in response to the reactions that <i id="mwAUY"><span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt167" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAUc" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt167" id="mwAUg"/></i> had triggered. The press release maintained support for the document, while attempting to clarify issues surrounding its reception and implementation.<sup about="#mwt171" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-38" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-38"}}'><a href="#cite_note-38" id="mwAUk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAUo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUs">[</span>38<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt174" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-39" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-39"}}'><a href="#cite_note-39" id="mwAU0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAU4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAU8">[</span>39<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVA">]</span></span></a></sup> The press release summarized the blessings that <i id="mwAVE"><span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt168" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAVI" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt168" id="mwAVM"/></i> was intended to permit as "short and simple pastoral blessings (neither liturgical nor ritualized) of couples in irregular situations (but not of their unions)".<sup about="#mwt177" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Clarifies_40-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Clarifies"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Clarifies-40"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Clarifies-40" id="mwAVQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAVU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVY">[</span>40<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVc">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAVg">Responding to episcopal conferences that restricted the issuing of blessings in their dioceses, the press release said that in some dioceses "it will be necessary not to introduce [blessings], while taking the time necessary for reading and interpretation", recognising the "strong cultural and even legal issues" that allowed bishops' restrictions to be "understood in their contexts". The press release emphasized that "pastoral blessings", which last "about 10 or 15 seconds", "[do] not justify anything that is not morally acceptable". It provided an example of a blessing a priest could use:<sup about="#mwt180" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:11_41-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":11"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:11-41"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:11-41" id="mwAVk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAVo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVs">[</span>41<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1244412712" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt181" id="mwAV0" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Blockquote/styles.css"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Quote","href":"./Template:Quote"},"params":{"text":{"wt":"Lord, look at these children of yours, grant them health, work, peace and mutual help.  Free them from everything that contradicts your Gospel and allow them to live according to your will. Amen."}},"i":0}}]}'>.mw-parser-output .templatequote{overflow:hidden;margin:1em 0;padding:0 32px}.mw-parser-output .templatequotecite{line-height:1.5em;text-align:left;margin-top:0}@media(min-width:500px){.mw-parser-output .templatequotecite{padding-left:1.6em}}</style><blockquote class="templatequote " about="#mwt181" id="mwAV4"><p>Lord, look at these children of yours, grant them health, work, peace and mutual help.  Free them from everything that contradicts your Gospel and allow them to live according to your will. Amen.</p></blockquote>
<p id="mwAV8">The press release clarified that blessings "must not take place in a prominent place within a sacred building, or in front of an altar, as this also would create confusion". It suggested some catechesis to "help everyone to understand that these types of blessings are not an endorsement of the life led by those who request them", reiterating that they are "simple expressions of pastoral closeness that do not impose the same requirements as a sacrament or a formal rite" and a priest that imparts such blessings "is not a heretic, he is not ratifying anything nor is he denying Catholic doctrine". The press release said that "this paternal gesture" could not be denied only because it was requested by "a great sinner".<sup about="#mwt183" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:11_41-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":11"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:11-41" id="mwAWA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAWE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWI">[</span>41<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="4" id="mwAWQ" aria-labelledby="Reaction"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Reaction">Reaction</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Fiducia_supplicans&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: Reaction"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<section data-mw-section-id="5" id="mwAWU" aria-labelledby="Supporters"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Supporters">Supporters</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Fiducia_supplicans&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: Supporters"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAWY">Several episcopal conferences supported the blessings in their jurisdictions, including the conferences of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Episcopal_Conference_of_Austria" title="Episcopal Conference of Austria" id="mwAWc">Austria</a>,<sup about="#mwt184" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-42" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-42"}}'><a href="#cite_note-42" id="mwAWg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAWk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWo">[</span>42<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWs">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt185" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-43" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-43"}}'><a href="#cite_note-43" id="mwAWw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAW0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAW4">[</span>43<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAW8">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt186" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-44" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-44"}}'><a href="#cite_note-44" id="mwAXA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAXE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXI">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXM">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bishops'_Conference_of_France" title="Bishops' Conference of France" id="mwAXQ">France</a>,<sup about="#mwt187" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-45" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-45"}}'><a href="#cite_note-45" id="mwAXU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAXY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXc">[</span>45<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt188" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-46" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-46"}}'><a href="#cite_note-46" id="mwAXk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAXo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXs">[</span>46<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt189" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-47" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-47"}}'><a href="#cite_note-47" id="mwAX0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAX4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAX8">[</span>47<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYA">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/German_Bishops'_Conference" title="German Bishops' Conference" id="mwAYE">Germany</a>,<sup about="#mwt192" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-48" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-48"}}'><a href="#cite_note-48" id="mwAYI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAYM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYQ">[</span>48<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYU">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt195" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-49" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-49"}}'><a href="#cite_note-49" id="mwAYY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAYc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYg">[</span>49<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYk">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Episcopal_Conference_of_Italy" title="Episcopal Conference of Italy" id="mwAYo">Italy</a>,<sup about="#mwt196" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-50" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-50"}}'><a href="#cite_note-50" id="mwAYs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAYw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAY0">[</span>50<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAY4">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maltese_Episcopal_Conference" title="Maltese Episcopal Conference" id="mwAY8">Malta</a>,<sup about="#mwt197" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-51" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-51"}}'><a href="#cite_note-51" id="mwAZA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZI">[</span>51<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZM">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Portuguese_Episcopal_Conference" title="Portuguese Episcopal Conference" id="mwAZQ">Portugal</a>,<sup about="#mwt198" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-52" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-52"}}'><a href="#cite_note-52" id="mwAZU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZc">[</span>52<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZg">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spanish_Episcopal_Conference" title="Spanish Episcopal Conference" id="mwAZk">Spain and Andorra</a>,<sup about="#mwt199" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-53" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-53"}}'><a href="#cite_note-53" id="mwAZo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZw">[</span>53<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZ0">]</span></span></a></sup> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Swiss_Bishops'_Conference" title="Swiss Bishops' Conference" id="mwAZ4">Switzerland</a>.<sup about="#mwt200" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-54" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-54"}}'><a href="#cite_note-54" id="mwAZ8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAaA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaE">[</span>54<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaI">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt201" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-55" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-55"}}'><a href="#cite_note-55" id="mwAaM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAaQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaU">[</span>55<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaY">]</span></span></a></sup> In Belgium, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Johan_Bonny" title="Johan Bonny" id="mwAac">Johan Bonny</a>, bishop of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_Catholic_Diocese_of_Antwerp" title="Roman Catholic Diocese of Antwerp" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAag">Antwerp</a>, praised the decision as "moving towards" future recognition of same-sex sacramental marriage in the Catholic Church.<sup about="#mwt204" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-56" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-56"}}'><a href="#cite_note-56" id="mwAak"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAao"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAas">[</span>56<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaw">]</span></span></a></sup> Geert De Kerpel, the spokesperson for the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholic_Church_in_Belgium" title="Catholic Church in Belgium" id="mwAa0">Belgian Catholic Church</a>, stated that it would not have an impact locally, as same-sex unions were already being blessed, but that the declaration would rightfully now apply this situation at an international level.<sup about="#mwt207" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-57" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-57"}}'><a href="#cite_note-57" id="mwAa4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAa8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbA">[</span>57<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbE">]</span></span></a></sup> According to Archbishop <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Franz_Lackner" title="Franz Lackner" id="mwAbI">Franz Lackner</a> of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_Catholic_Archdiocese_of_Salzburg" title="Roman Catholic Archdiocese of Salzburg" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAbM">Salzburg</a>, the document basically means that "one can no longer say no" to blessing same-sex unions.<sup about="#mwt210" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:1_58-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":1"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:1-58"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:1-58" id="mwAbQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAbU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbY">[</span>58<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbc">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAbg"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_J._Martin_(priest)" title="James J. Martin (priest)" id="mwAbk">James Martin</a>, an American <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jesuit" title="Jesuit" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAbo">Jesuit</a> priest, called the declaration "a major step forward in the church's ministry to LGBTQ people" and demonstrated the church affirming same-sex couples' desire "for God's presence and help in their committed and loving relationships".<sup about="#mwt211" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:10_12-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":10"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:10-12" id="mwAbs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAbw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAb0">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAb4">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt212" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:7_59-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:7-59" id="mwAb8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAcA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcE">[</span>59<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcI">]</span></span></a></sup> The next day, he blessed a couple who had been married in a non-Catholic church.<sup about="#mwt215" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:7_59-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":7"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:7-59"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:7-59" id="mwAcM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAcQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcU">[</span>59<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcY">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt218" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-60" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-60"}}'><a href="#cite_note-60" id="mwAcc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAcg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAck">[</span>60<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAco">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAcs">In <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/India" title="India" id="mwAcw">India</a>, Archbishop <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oswald_Gracias" title="Oswald Gracias" id="mwAc0">Oswald Gracias</a> of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_Catholic_Archdiocese_of_Bombay" title="Roman Catholic Archdiocese of Bombay" id="mwAc4">Bombay</a> – one of Francis's <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Council_of_Cardinals" title="Council of Cardinals" id="mwAc8">cardinal advisers</a> – expressed that seeking and bestowing blessings is a deeply ingrained custom in India, describing it as a "natural" practice that resonates with the spirituality of the region and deeming it "an affirmation of our spirituality and a gift." While Gracias clarified that the Church's stance on the doctrine of marriage remains unchanged, emphasizing that <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt219" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAdA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt219" id="mwAdE"/> does not signify a move toward recognizing same-sex unions as sacramental marriages, he highlighted the alignment of <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt220" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAdI" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt220" id="mwAdM"/> with his own pastoral approach toward the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LGBT_community" title="LGBT community" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAdQ">LGBTQ+ community</a>.<sup about="#mwt225" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-61" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-61"}}'><a href="#cite_note-61" id="mwAdU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAdY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAdc">[</span>61<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAdg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt228" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-62" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-62"}}'><a href="#cite_note-62" id="mwAdk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAdo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAds">[</span>62<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAdw">]</span></span></a></sup> Archbishop <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Victor_Lyngdoh" title="Victor Lyngdoh" id="mwAd0">Victor Lyngdoh</a> of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_Catholic_Archdiocese_of_Shillong" title="Roman Catholic Archdiocese of Shillong" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAd4">Shillong</a>, issued a letter to the clergy and followers, reiterating the sentiments of <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt221" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAd8" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt221" id="mwAeA"/>. While he underscored that the blessing should not be misconstrued as the Church's blessings conferred during marriage, he drew on Francis's urging of the faithful to "avoid being 'judges who only deny, reject, and exclude<span style="padding-right:.15em;" about="#mwt222" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAeE" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"&apos; \"","href":"./Template:&apos;_\""},"params":{},"i":0}}]}'>'</span><span about="#mwt222">"</span>.<sup about="#mwt231" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-63" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-63"}}'><a href="#cite_note-63" id="mwAeI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAeM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAeQ">[</span>63<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAeU">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt234" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-64" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-64"}}'><a href="#cite_note-64" id="mwAeY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAec"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAeg">[</span>64<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAek">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAeo">The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_Conference_of_Catholic_Bishops" title="United States Conference of Catholic Bishops" id="mwAes">United States Conference of Catholic Bishops</a> (USCCB) issued a statement saying the declaration made "a distinction between <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Liturgical" title="Liturgical" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAew">liturgical</a> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sacraments_of_the_Catholic_Church" title="Sacraments of the Catholic Church" id="mwAe0">sacramental</a>) blessings, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pastoral_care" title="Pastoral care" id="mwAe4">pastoral</a> blessings",<sup about="#mwt237" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:62_65-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":62"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:62-65"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:62-65" id="mwAe8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAfA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAfE">[</span>65<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAfI">]</span></span></a></sup> and that the declaration affirmed that "[t]he Church's teaching on marriage has not changed".<sup about="#mwt238" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:62_65-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":62"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:62-65" id="mwAfM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAfQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAfU">[</span>65<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAfY">]</span></span></a></sup> American bishop <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_Barron" title="Robert Barron" id="mwAfc">Robert Barron</a> – who serves as the chairman of the USCCB's <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Committee_on_Laity,_Marriage,_Family_Life,_and_Youth" title="Committee on Laity, Marriage, Family Life, and Youth" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAfg">Committee on Laity, Marriage, Family Life, and Youth</a> – said the declaration did not demonstrate a change in Catholic doctrine on sexuality and marriage.<sup about="#mwt241" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-USCCB_66-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"USCCB"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-USCCB-66"}}'><a href="#cite_note-USCCB-66" id="mwAfk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAfo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAfs">[</span>66<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAfw">]</span></span></a></sup> The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canadian_Conference_of_Catholic_Bishops" title="Canadian Conference of Catholic Bishops" id="mwAf0">Canadian Conference of Catholic Bishops</a> also said that the document affirms church teaching on marriage "explicitly".<sup about="#mwt244" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:2_67-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":2"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:2-67"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:2-67" id="mwAf4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAf8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAgA">[</span>67<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAgE">]</span></span></a></sup> Danish bishop <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Czeslaw_Kozon" title="Czeslaw Kozon" id="mwAgI">Czeslaw Kozon</a> said that there was a problem not with the content of the statement "but the way it will be received and interpreted".<sup about="#mwt247" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:72_68-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":72"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:72-68"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:72-68" id="mwAgM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAgQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAgU">[</span>68<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAgY">]</span></span></a></sup> The president of the Ghana Catholic Bishops' Conference, Bishop <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matthew_Kwasi_Gyamfi" title="Matthew Kwasi Gyamfi" id="mwAgc">Matthew Kwasi Gyamfi</a> said: "What people do not understand is that if a gay couple goes to the priest to bless them, and the pope says yes, you are blessing the people and not the union".<sup about="#mwt248" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:72_68-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":72"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:72-68" id="mwAgg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAgk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAgo">[</span>68<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAgs">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="6" id="mwAgw" aria-labelledby="Criticism"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Criticism">Criticism</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Fiducia_supplicans&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Criticism"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAg0">The declaration sparked considerable controversy and criticism among Catholics, including from several conservative commentators, clerical congregations, and high-profile bishops, priests, and lay people.<sup about="#mwt249" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:72_68-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":72"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:72-68" id="mwAg4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAg8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhA">[</span>68<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhE">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt252" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ap-imray-23_69-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ap-imray-23"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-ap-imray-23-69"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ap-imray-23-69" id="mwAhI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAhM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhQ">[</span>69<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhU">]</span></span></a></sup> Several episcopal conferences barred the blessings in their jurisdictions or asked priests to refrain from them, including the conferences of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Episcopal_Conference_of_Benin" title="Episcopal Conference of Benin" id="mwAhY">Benin</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Episcopal_Conference_of_the_Congo" title="Episcopal Conference of the Congo" id="mwAhc">Congo-Brazzaville</a>,<sup about="#mwt255" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:12_70-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":12"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:12-70"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:12-70" id="mwAhg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAhk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAho">[</span>70<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhs">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hungarian_Catholic_Bishops'_Conference" title="Hungarian Catholic Bishops' Conference" id="mwAhw">Hungary</a>,<sup about="#mwt258" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-71" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-71"}}'><a href="#cite_note-71" id="mwAh0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAh4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAh8">[</span>71<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAiA">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Episcopal_Conference_of_Malawi" title="Episcopal Conference of Malawi" id="mwAiE">Malawi</a>,<sup about="#mwt261" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-72" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-72"}}'><a href="#cite_note-72" id="mwAiI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAiM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAiQ">[</span>72<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAiU">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Namibian_Catholic_Bishops'_Conference" title="Namibian Catholic Bishops' Conference" id="mwAiY">Namibia</a>,<sup about="#mwt262" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:13_73-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":13"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:13-73" id="mwAic"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAig"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAik">[</span>73<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAio">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Episcopal_Conference_of_Togo" title="Episcopal Conference of Togo" id="mwAis">Togo</a>,<sup about="#mwt263" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:12_70-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":12"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:12-70" id="mwAiw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAi0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAi4">[</span>70<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAi8">]</span></span></a></sup> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zambia_Conference_of_Catholic_Bishops" title="Zambia Conference of Catholic Bishops" id="mwAjA">Zambia</a>.<sup about="#mwt266" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:13_73-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":13"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:13-73"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:13-73" id="mwAjE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAjI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAjM">[</span>73<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAjQ">]</span></span></a></sup> The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Polish_Episcopal_Conference" title="Polish Episcopal Conference" id="mwAjU">Polish Episcopal Conference</a> suggested only blessing "individual people living in complete abstinence".<sup about="#mwt269" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-74" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-74"}}'><a href="#cite_note-74" id="mwAjY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAjc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAjg">[</span>74<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAjk">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt272" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-75" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-75"}}'><a href="#cite_note-75" id="mwAjo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAjs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAjw">[</span>75<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAj0">]</span></span></a></sup> On 11 January 2024, Cardinal <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fridolin_Ambongo_Besungu" title="Fridolin Ambongo Besungu" id="mwAj4">Fridolin Ambongo Besungu</a> announced that all episcopal conferences in Africa, represented in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Symposium_of_Episcopal_Conferences_of_Africa_and_Madagascar" title="Symposium of Episcopal Conferences of Africa and Madagascar" id="mwAj8">SECAM</a>, would reject blessings for same-sex couples, stating that "the extra-liturgical blessings proposed in the declaration...cannot be carried out in Africa without exposing themselves to scandals."<sup about="#mwt275" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:4_76-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":4"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:4-76"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:4-76" id="mwAkA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAkE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAkI">[</span>76<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAkM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAkQ">Forbidding blessings in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_Catholic_Archdiocese_of_Mary_Most_Holy_in_Astana" title="Roman Catholic Archdiocese of Mary Most Holy in Astana" id="mwAkU">Archdiocese of Mary Most Holy in Astana</a> in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kazakhstan" title="Kazakhstan" id="mwAkY">Kazakhstan</a>, Archbishop <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tomasz_Peta" title="Tomasz Peta" id="mwAkc">Tomasz Peta</a> and Auxiliary Bishop <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Athanasius_Schneider" title="Athanasius Schneider" id="mwAkg">Athanasius Schneider</a> denounced <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt276" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAkk" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt276" id="mwAko"/> for contradicting "<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Revelation" title="Revelation" id="mwAks">divine revelation</a> and the uninterrupted, bimillennial doctrine and practice of the Catholic Church".<sup about="#mwt281" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-77" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-77"}}'><a href="#cite_note-77" id="mwAkw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAk0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAk4">[</span>77<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAk8">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt284" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Catholic_Herald_78-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Catholic Herald"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Catholic_Herald-78"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Catholic_Herald-78" id="mwAlA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAlE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAlI">[</span>78<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAlM">]</span></span></a></sup> Schneider added that <span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt277" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAlQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt277" id="mwAlU"/> was a "great deception" and warned of "evil that resides in the very permission to bless<span class="nowrap" about="#mwt278" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAlY" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"nbsp","href":"./Template:Nbsp"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}'><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></span>[...] same-sex couples".<sup about="#mwt285" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ap-imray-23_69-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ap-imray-23"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ap-imray-23-69" id="mwAlc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAlg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAlk">[</span>69<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAlo">]</span></span></a></sup> German cardinal <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gerhard_Ludwig_Müller" title="Gerhard Ludwig Müller" id="mwAls">Gerhard Ludwig Müller</a> said that the declaration was "sacrilegious and blasphemous",<sup about="#mwt288" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-79" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-79"}}'><a href="#cite_note-79" id="mwAlw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAl0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAl4">[</span>79<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAl8">]</span></span></a></sup> and that the Catholic Church "cannot celebrate one thing and teach another".<sup about="#mwt289" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ap-imray-23_69-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ap-imray-23"}}'><a
