href="#cite_note-ap-imray-23-69" id="mwAmA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAmE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmI">[</span>69<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmM">]</span></span></a></sup> Cardinal <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_Sarah" title="Robert Sarah" id="mwAmQ">Robert Sarah</a>, former <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prefect" title="Prefect" id="mwAmU">prefect</a> of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Congregation_for_Divine_Worship_and_the_Discipline_of_the_Sacraments" title="Congregation for Divine Worship and the Discipline of the Sacraments" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAmY">Congregation for Divine Worship and the Discipline of the Sacraments</a>, called the declaration "a heresy that seriously undermines the Church".<sup about="#mwt292" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-80" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-80"}}'><a href="#cite_note-80" id="mwAmc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAmg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmk">[</span>80<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmo">]</span></span></a></sup> Cardinal <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joseph_Zen" title="Joseph Zen" id="mwAms">Joseph Zen</a> criticized parts of the declaration as "an absolute subjective error" and suggested that Fernández should resign.<sup about="#mwt295" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-81" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-81"}}'><a href="#cite_note-81" id="mwAmw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAm0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAm4">[</span>81<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAm8">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAnA">A Spanish-language petition requesting that the declaration be rescinded led Cardinal <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/José_Cobo_Cano" title="José Cobo Cano" id="mwAnE">José Cobo Cano</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_Catholic_Archdiocese_of_Madrid" title="Roman Catholic Archdiocese of Madrid" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAnI">Archbishop of Madrid</a>, to threaten disciplinary action against any clergy in his diocese that signs it.<sup about="#mwt296" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:4_76-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":4"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:4-76" id="mwAnM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAnQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAnU">[</span>76<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAnY">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAnc"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sviatoslav_Shevchuk" title="Sviatoslav Shevchuk" id="mwAng">Sviatoslav Shevchuk</a>, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ukrainian_Catholic_Major_Archeparchy_of_Kyiv–Galicia" title="Ukrainian Catholic Major Archeparchy of Kyiv–Galicia" id="mwAnk">Major Archbishop of Kyiv–Galicia</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Primate_(bishop)" title="Primate (bishop)" id="mwAno">Primate</a> of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ukrainian_Greek_Catholic_Church" title="Ukrainian Greek Catholic Church" id="mwAns">Ukrainian Greek Catholic Church</a>, said that the declaration concerns purely the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_Church" title="Latin Church" id="mwAnw">Latin Church</a> and had no legal force in 23 autonomous <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eastern_Catholic_Churches" title="Eastern Catholic Churches" id="mwAn0">Eastern Catholic Churches</a>.<sup about="#mwt299" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-82" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-82"}}'><a href="#cite_note-82" id="mwAn4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAn8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoA">[</span>82<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoE">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt302" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:02_83-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":02"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:02-83"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:02-83" id="mwAoI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAoM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoQ">[</span>83<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoU">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAoY">Several figures associated with <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Traditionalist_Catholicism" title="Traditionalist Catholicism" id="mwAoc">traditionalist Catholicism</a> rejected the declaration, including Archbishop <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carlo_Maria_Viganò" title="Carlo Maria Viganò" id="mwAog">Carlo Maria Viganò</a>, former <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apostolic_Nunciature_to_the_United_States" title="Apostolic Nunciature to the United States" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAok">Apostolic Nuncio to the United States</a>.<sup about="#mwt305" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-84" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-84"}}'><a href="#cite_note-84" id="mwAoo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAos"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAow">[</span>84<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAo0">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="7" id="mwAo4" aria-labelledby="Christians_outside_the_Catholic_Church"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Christians_outside_the_Catholic_Church">Christians outside the Catholic Church</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Fiducia_supplicans&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Christians outside the Catholic Church"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAo8">American evangelist <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Franklin_Graham" title="Franklin Graham" id="mwApA">Franklin Graham</a>,<sup about="#mwt308" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-85" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-85"}}'><a href="#cite_note-85" id="mwApE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwApI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwApM">[</span>85<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwApQ">]</span></span></a></sup> and the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Southern_Baptist_Theological_Seminary" title="Southern Baptist Theological Seminary" id="mwApU">Southern Baptist Theological Seminary</a> president <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albert_Mohler" title="Albert Mohler" id="mwApY">Albert Mohler</a> were critical.<sup about="#mwt311" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-86" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-86"}}'><a href="#cite_note-86" id="mwApc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwApg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwApk">[</span>86<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwApo">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carl_Trueman" title="Carl Trueman" id="mwAps">Carl Trueman</a> expressed concern that contemporary conservative Protestants in the West would be less able to shelter under the Roman Catholic Church's cultural umbrella as a result of <i id="mwApw">Fiducia supplicans</i>.<sup about="#mwt314" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-87" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-87"}}'><a href="#cite_note-87" id="mwAp0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAp4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAp8">[</span>87<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAqA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAqE">On 20 February 2024,<sup about="#mwt318" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:18_88-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":18"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:18-88"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:18-88" id="mwAqI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAqM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAqQ">[</span>88<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAqU">]</span></span></a></sup> the Synodal Biblical-Theological Commission of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moscow_Patriarchate" title="Moscow Patriarchate" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAqY">Moscow Patriarchate</a> called <i id="mwAqc"><span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt315" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAqg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt315" id="mwAqk"/></i> an "innovation [that] reflected a sharp departure from Christian moral teaching".<sup about="#mwt319" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:18_88-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":18"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:18-88" id="mwAqo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAqs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAqw">[</span>88<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAq0">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt322" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:19_89-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":19"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:19-89"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:19-89" id="mwAq4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAq8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArA">[</span>89<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArE">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt325" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-90" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-90"}}'><a href="#cite_note-90" id="mwArI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwArM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArQ">[</span>90<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArU">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwArY">On 7 March 2024, the Holy Synod of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Coptic_Orthodox_Church" title="Coptic Orthodox Church" id="mwArc">Coptic Orthodox Church</a> published a statement condemning homosexual activity and relationships as contrary to Christian morality, and suspending ecumenical dialogue with the Roman Catholic Church pending review. While the statement did not reference <i id="mwArg"><span title="Latin-language text" about="#mwt326" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwArk" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"la"},"2":{"wt":"Fiducia supplicans"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="la">Fiducia supplicans</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Latin-language_text" about="#mwt326" id="mwAro"/></i>,<sup about="#mwt329" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-OCP_91-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"OCP"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-OCP-91"}}'><a href="#cite_note-OCP-91" id="mwArs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwArw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAr0">[</span>91<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAr4">]</span></span></a></sup> Catholic publications linked the suspension of ecumenical dialogue to the declaration.<sup about="#mwt330" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:18_88-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":18"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:18-88" id="mwAr8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAsA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAsE">[</span>88<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAsI">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt333" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-92" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-92"}}'><a href="#cite_note-92" id="mwAsM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAsQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAsU">[</span>92<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAsY">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt334" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:19_89-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":19"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:19-89" id="mwAsc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAsg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAsk">[</span>89<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAso">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="8" id="mwAss" aria-labelledby="Aftermath"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Aftermath">Aftermath</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Fiducia_supplicans&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Aftermath"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAsw">At a closed-door meeting with 800 Roman clergy on 13 January 2024, Francis stated that homosexual individuals could be blessed, but not LGBT organizations. He added the reason why the measure on the blessings of same-sex couples was rejected in Africa was that "[t]he culture [in Africa] does not accept it". He added as a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comparison" title="Comparison" id="mwAs0">comparison</a> on blessing homosexuals: "When we bless an <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Entrepreneurship" title="Entrepreneurship" id="mwAs4">entrepreneur</a>, we do not ask if he has stolen".<sup about="#mwt337" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-93" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-93"}}'><a href="#cite_note-93" id="mwAs8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAtA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAtE">[</span>93<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAtI">]</span></span></a></sup> The next day, Francis answered questions on <i id="mwAtM">Fiducia supplicans</i> in an interview on <i id="mwAtQ"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Che_tempo_che_fa" title="Che tempo che fa" id="mwAtU">Che tempo che fa</a></i>, saying that "the Lord blesses everyone who is capable of being baptised, that is, every person", and that such blessings invite people "to see what the road is that the Lord proposes to them".<sup about="#mwt340" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-94" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-94"}}'><a href="#cite_note-94" id="mwAtY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAtc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAtg">[</span>94<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAtk">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAto">On 26 January 2024, addressing the DDF's annual plenary assembly, Francis said that the purpose of the blessings discussed in the document was to "concretely show the closeness of the Lord and the Church to all those who, finding themselves in different situations, ask for help to continue – sometimes to begin – a journey of faith". He emphasised that non-liturgical blessings "do not require moral perfection to be received" and that they are bestowed on the people requesting them, not their union.<sup about="#mwt343" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-95" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-95"}}'><a href="#cite_note-95" id="mwAts"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAtw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAt0">[</span>95<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAt4">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt346" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-96" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-96"}}'><a href="#cite_note-96" id="mwAt8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAuA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuE">[</span>96<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuI">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt349" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-97" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-97"}}'><a href="#cite_note-97" id="mwAuM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAuQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuU">[</span>97<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuY">]</span></span></a></sup> On 29 January 2024, in an interview with <i id="mwAuc"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_Stampa" title="La Stampa" id="mwAug">La Stampa</a></i>, Francis highlighted the "spirit of the declaration" that "aims to include, not divide", in contrast to those that would want to "make a list of sinners who can enter the Church and a list of sinners who cannot be in the Church". Francis said that "those who vehemently protest [the document] belong to small ideological groups", while the church in Africa presented "a special case" because "for them, homosexuality is something 'ugly' from a cultural point of view".<sup about="#mwt352" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-98" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-98"}}'><a href="#cite_note-98" id="mwAuk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAuo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAus">[</span>98<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAu0">When asked about blessings for homosexual couples, in an interview with <i id="mwAu4"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/60_Minutes" title="60 Minutes" id="mwAu8">60 Minutes</a></i>, Pope Francis stated: "What I allowed was not to bless the union, that cannot be done because that is not the sacrament. But to bless each person, yes, the blessing is for everyone."<sup about="#mwt355" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-99" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-99"}}'><a href="#cite_note-99" id="mwAvA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAvE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvI">[</span>99<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="9" id="mwAvQ" aria-labelledby="See_also"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="See_also">See also</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Fiducia_supplicans&amp;action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: See also"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<li about="#cite_note-71" id="cite_note-71" data-mw-footnote-number="71"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwBfE"><a href="#cite_ref-71" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwBfI"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwBfM">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-71" class="mw-reference-text reference-text"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1333433106" about="#mwt256" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwBfQ" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Citation/CS1/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Cite web ","href":"./Template:Cite_web"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"27 December 2023"},"title":{"wt":"A Magyar Katolikus Püspöki Konferencia (MKPK) közleménye az áldásokról"},"trans-title":{"wt":"Announcement of the Hungarian Catholic Bishops&apos; Conference (MKPK) on blessings"},"url":{"wt":"https://www.magyarkurir.hu/hirek/a-magyar-katolikus-puspoki-konferencia-mkpk-kozlemenye-az-aldasokrol"},"access-date":{"wt":"2 January 2024"},"website":{"wt":"Magyar Kurír"},"language":{"wt":"hu"},"quote":{"wt":"Hazánk lelkipásztori helyzetét mérlegelve a Püspöki Konferencia útmutatásként fogalmazza meg a lelkipásztorok számára, hogy nemi identitásától és szexuális orientációjától függetlenül egyénenként minden embert megáldhatunk, mindig kerülni kell azonban azt, hogy a pusztán párkapcsolatban együtt élő, az egyházilag nem érvényes házasságban vagy egynemű párkapcsolatban élő párok számára közös áldást adjunk."},"trans-quote":{"wt":"Considering the pastoral situation in our country, the Episcopal Conference formulates guidance for pastors, stating that we can bless each person individually, regardless of their gender identity and sexual orientation. Retrieved <span class="nowrap" id="mwBfs">2 January</span> 2024</span>. <q id="mwBfw">Hazánk lelkipásztori helyzetét mérlegelve a Püspöki Konferencia útmutatásként fogalmazza meg a lelkipásztorok számára, hogy nemi identitásától és szexuális orientációjától függetlenül egyénenként minden embert megáldhatunk, mindig kerülni kell azonban azt, hogy a pusztán párkapcsolatban együtt élő, az egyházilag nem érvényes házasságban vagy egynemű párkapcsolatban élő párok számára közös áldást adjunk.</q> <span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBf0">[</span>Considering the pastoral situation in our country, the Episcopal Conference formulates guidance for pastors, stating that we can bless each person individually, regardless of their gender identity and sexual orientation. However, it is always necessary to avoid giving a joint blessing to couples living together in non-church-sanctioned marriages or same-sex partnerships.<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBf4">]</span></cite><span title="ctx_ver=Z39.88-2004&amp;rft_val_fmt=info%3Aofi%2Ffmt%3Akev%3Amtx%3Ajournal&amp;rft.genre=unknown&amp;rft.jtitle=Magyar+Kur%C3%ADr&amp;rft.atitle=A+Magyar+Katolikus+P%C3%BCsp%C3%B6ki+Konferencia+%28MKPK%29+k%C3%B6zlem%C3%A9nye+az+%C3%A1ld%C3%A1sokr%C3%B3l&amp;rft.date=2023-12-27&amp;rft_id=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.magyarkurir.hu%2Fhirek%2Fa-magyar-katolikus-puspoki-konferencia-mkpk-kozlemenye-az-aldasokrol&amp;rfr_id=info%3Asid%2Fen.wikipedia.org%3AFiducia+supplicans" class="Z3988" about="#mwt256" id="mwBf8"></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:CS1_Hungarian-language_sources_(hu)" about="#mwt256" id="mwBgA"/></span></li>

<li about="#cite_note-74" id="cite_note-74" data-mw-footnote-number="74"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwBh0"><a href="#cite_ref-74" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwBh4"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwBh8">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-74" class="mw-reference-text reference-text"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1333433106" about="#mwt267" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwBiA" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Citation/CS1/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Cite web ","href":"./Template:Cite_web"},"params":{"last":{"wt":"Gęsiak SJ"},"first":{"wt":"Leszek"},"date":{"wt":"21 December 2023"},"title":{"wt":"Rzecznik Episkopatu: Deklaracja Fiducia supplicans w niczym nie zmienia dotychczasowego nauczania Kościoła"},"url":{"wt":"https://episkopat.pl/rzecznik-episkopatu-deklaracja-fiducia-supplicans-w-niczym-nie-zmienia-dotychczasowego-nauczania-kosciola/"},"access-date":{"wt":"7 January 2024"},"website":{"wt":"[[Polish Episcopal Conference]]"},"language":{"wt":"pl-PL"},"quote":{"wt":"Zarówno Deklaracja jak i Nota stwierdzają, że \"nie wyklucza [się] udzielania błogosławieństwa pojedynczym osobom o skłonnościach homoseksualnych, które przejawiają pragnienie życia w wierności objawionym planom Bożym, tak jak naucza Kościół\". Chodzi zatem o pojedyncze osoby żyjące w całkowitej wstrzemięźliwości."},"trans-quote":{"wt":"Both the Declaration and the Note state that \"the blessing of individual persons with homosexual tendencies who manifest the desire to live in fidelity to the revealed plans of God, as taught by the Church, is not excluded.\" Therefore, we are talking about individual people living in complete abstinence."}},"i":0}}]}'/><cite id="CITEREFGęsiak_SJ2023" class="citation web cs1 cs1-prop-foreign-lang-source" about="#mwt267">Gęsiak SJ, Leszek (21 December 2023). <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://episkopat.pl/rzecznik-episkopatu-deklaracja-fiducia-supplicans-w-niczym-nie-zmienia-dotychczasowego-nauczania-kosciola/" class="external text" id="mwBiE">"Rzecznik Episkopatu: Deklaracja Fiducia supplicans w niczym nie zmienia dotychczasowego nauczania Kościoła"</a>. <i id="mwBiI"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Polish_Episcopal_Conference" title="Polish Episcopal Conference" id="mwBiM">Polish Episcopal Conference</a></i> (in Polish)<span class="reference-accessdate" id="mwBiQ">. Chodzi zatem o pojedyncze osoby żyjące w całkowitej wstrzemięźliwości.</q> <span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBic">[</span>Both the Declaration and the Note state that "the blessing of individual persons with homosexual tendencies who manifest the desire to live in fidelity to the revealed plans of God, as taught by the Church, is not excluded." Therefore, we are talking about individual people living in complete abstinence.<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBig">]</span></cite><span title="ctx_ver=Z39.88-2004&amp;rft_val_fmt=info%3Aofi%2Ffmt%3Akev%3Amtx%3Ajournal&amp;rft.genre=unknown&amp;rft.jtitle=Polish+Episcopal+Conference&amp;rft.atitle=Rzecznik+Episkopatu%3A+Deklaracja+Fiducia+supplicans+w+niczym+nie+zmienia+dotychczasowego+nauczania+Ko%C5%9Bcio%C5%82a&amp;rft.date=2023-12-21&amp;rft.aulast=G%C4%99siak+SJ&amp;rft.aufirst=Leszek&amp;rft_id=https%3A%2F%2Fepiskopat.pl%2Frzecznik-episkopatu-deklaracja-fiducia-supplicans-w-niczym-nie-zmienia-dotychczasowego-nauczania-kosciola%2F&amp;rfr_id=info%3Asid%2Fen.wikipedia.org%3AFiducia+supplicans" class="Z3988" about="#mwt267" id="mwBik"></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:CS1_Polish-language_sources_(pl)" about="#mwt267" id="mwBio"/></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-75" id="cite_note-75" data-mw-footnote-number="75"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwBis"><a href="#cite_ref-75" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwBiw"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwBi0">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-75" class="mw-reference-text reference-text"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1333433106" about="#mwt270" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwBi4" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Citation/CS1/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Cite web ","href":"./Template:Cite_web"},"params":{"last":{"wt":"Arnold"},"first":{"wt":"Tyler"},"date":{"wt":"22 December 2023"},"title":{"wt":"Polish bishops: Church does not have authority to &apos;bless same-sex unions&apos;"},"url":{"wt":"https://www.ewtnnews.com/world/europe/polish-bishops-church-does-not-have-authority-to-bless-same-sex-unions"},"access-date":{"wt":"7 January 2024"},"website":{"wt":"[[Catholic News Agency]]"},"language":{"wt":"en"},"quote":{"wt":"Catholic bishops in Poland have ruled out the possibility of blessing \"same-sex unions\" but remain open to blessing individuals with homosexual tendencies, only if they are \"living in complete abstinence\" of sexual activities. Retrieved <span class="nowrap" id="mwBow">7 January</span> 2024</span>. <q id="mwBo0">Several Orthodox leaders, including Metropolitan Hilarion (Alfeyev), formerly the Russian Orthodox Church's Foreign Minister and now Metropolitan of Budapest, have also strongly criticised Fiducia Supplicans, while the Primate of the Greek Catholic Church in Ukraine, Major Archbishop Svetoslav Shevchuk, said in a statement last week that the Declaration would not apply to the world's 23 autonomous Eastern Rite Catholic confessions, which followed separate canonical rules.</q></cite><span title="ctx_ver=Z39.88-2004&amp;rft_val_fmt=info%3Aofi%2Ffmt%3Akev%3Amtx%3Ajournal&amp;rft.genre=unknown&amp;rft.jtitle=Church+Times&amp;rft.atitle=Episcopate+gives+Rome+a+rough+ride+over+Fiducia+Supplicans+declaration&amp;rft.date=2024-01-05&amp;rft.aulast=Luxmoore&amp;rft.aufirst=Jonathan&amp;rft_id=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.churchtimes.co.uk%2Farticles%2F2024%2F5-january%2Fnews%2Fworld%2Fepiscopate-gives-rome-a-rough-ride-over-fiducia-supplicans-declaration&amp;rfr_id=info%3Asid%2Fen.wikipedia.org%3AFiducia+supplicans" class="Z3988" about="#mwt300" id="mwBo4"></span></span></li>

Retrieved <span class="nowrap" id="mwBpg">7 January</span> 2024</span>. <q id="mwBpk">The delirious Declaration Fiducia Supplicans [...] pierces the veil of hypocrisy and deception of the Bergoglian Hierarchy, showing these false shepherds for what they really are: servants of Satan and his most zealous allies, beginning with the usurper who sits - an abomination of desolation - on the Throne of Peter.</q></cite><span title="ctx_ver=Z39.88-2004&amp;rft_val_fmt=info%3Aofi%2Ffmt%3Akev%3Amtx%3Ajournal&amp;rft.genre=unknown&amp;rft.jtitle=EXSURGE+DOMINE&amp;rft.atitle=Declaration+on+%22Fiducia+supplicans%22&amp;rft.date=2023-12-21&amp;rft.aulast=Vigan%C3%B2&amp;rft.aufirst=Carlo+Maria&amp;rft_id=https%3A%2F%2Fexsurgedomine.it%2Fen%2F231220-fiducia-supplicans-eng%2F&amp;rfr_id=info%3Asid%2Fen.wikipedia.org%3AFiducia+supplicans" class="Z3988" about="#mwt303" id="mwBpo"></span></span></li>
